     g) (−14a³ + 21a²) ÷ 7a             h) (9x³y² − 6x²y³) ÷ (−3x²y²)
     i) (x² + 5x + 6) ÷ (x + 2)         j) (x² − 2x − 24) ÷ (x − 6)
     k) (x² − 49) ÷ (x + 7)             l) (2x² + 7x + 3) ÷ (x + 3)
     m) (3x² − 5x − 2) ÷ (x − 2)        n) (x² − 10x + 25) ÷ (x − 5)
     o) (6x² + 11x + 3) ÷ (2x + 3)      p) (9a² − 4) ÷ (3a − 2)
     q) (5x² + 13x − 6) ÷ (5x − 2)      r) (x³ + 2x² + 3x + 6) ÷ (x + 2)
     s) (4x² − 12x + 9) ÷ (2x − 3)      t) (x³ − 9x) ÷ (x² + 3x)
     Answers: a) 3x² − 2x + 1   b) 3a − 2b   c) 2m + 3   d) 4p − 1
              e) 2x + 3y − 1   f) 3y² − 2y + 1   g) −2a² + 3a   h) −3x + 2y
              i) x + 3   j) x + 4   k) x − 7   l) 2x + 1   m) 3x + 1   n) x − 5
              o) 3x + 1   p) 3a + 2   q) x + 3   r) x² + 3   s) 2x − 3
              t) x − 3

TEACHING TIPS
  Struggling: always expand first to see the pattern backwards (make a list:
  (x + 2)(x + 5) = x² + 7x + 10 and so on) and use algebra tiles or area
  rectangles to "build" x² + 5x + 6 as a rectangle; practise finding factor
  pairs of numbers up to 100 quickly, with their sums and differences; insist on
  checking every answer by expanding. Harder: factorise
  a³ − b³ = (a − b)(a² + ab + b²) and a³ + b³;
  x⁴ − 5x² + 4 = (x − 1)(x + 1)(x − 2)(x + 2); expressions such as
  x² + 2xy + y² − 9 = (x + y + 3)(x + y − 3); find all whole numbers k for which
  x² + kx + 12 factorises (k = ±7, ±8, ±13); prove that the difference of the
  squares of two consecutive odd numbers is a multiple of 8.


================================================================================
TOPIC 6.9 — ALGEBRAIC FRACTIONS: SIMPLIFYING, ADDING, SUBTRACTING
================================================================================
WHAT THE CHILD MUST LEARN
  - An ALGEBRAIC FRACTION has an algebraic expression in the numerator or the
    denominator (or both): 3/x, (x + 1)/(x − 4), 2a/(3b²). It works exactly like
    a number fraction, because the letters stand for numbers.
  - The DENOMINATOR CAN NEVER BE ZERO. (x + 1)/(x − 4) has no value when x = 4,
    so we say x ≠ 4. Find the forbidden values by solving denominator = 0
    (factorise it if needed).
  - SIMPLIFY by cancelling COMMON FACTORS of the numerator and denominator:
    6x²y/(9xy³) = 2x/(3y²). If there are sums, FACTORISE FIRST, then cancel
    whole brackets:
    (x² − 9)/(x² + 5x + 6) = (x + 3)(x − 3)/((x + 2)(x + 3)) = (x − 3)/(x + 2).
  - You may cancel FACTORS, never TERMS: (x + 6)/6 is NOT x + 1 or x. Test any
    "cancelling" with a number, e.g. x = 6: (6 + 6)/6 = 2.
  - Useful fact: (b − a) = −(a − b), so (a − b)/(b − a) = −1.
  - ADDING and SUBTRACTING: with the SAME denominator, add or subtract the
    numerators: (2x + 1)/5 − (x − 3)/5 = (2x + 1 − x + 3)/5 = (x + 4)/5. Put
    brackets round a numerator that is being subtracted.
  - With DIFFERENT denominators use the LCD (lowest common denominator), as with
    numbers: x/3 + x/4 = 4x/12 + 3x/12 = 7x/12; 1/a + 1/b = (b + a)/(ab);
    3/(x + 2) − 2/(x + 3) = (3(x + 3) − 2(x + 2))/((x + 2)(x + 3))
    = (x + 5)/((x + 2)(x + 3)).
  - MULTIPLYING: factorise, cancel, then multiply numerators and denominators.
    DIVIDING: multiply by the reciprocal of the second fraction
    (keep-change-flip), then simplify.
  - CHECK any answer by putting in a number for the letter (not one that makes a
    denominator zero): both sides must give the same value.

PART A — DESCRIPTIVE
--------------------
D1. The time (in hours) to drive 120 km at a speed of v km/h is 120/v. Find the
    time when v = 40 and v = 60. Then say for which value of x the fraction
    (x + 1)/(x − 4) has no value, and why.
    Answer: 3 hours and 2 hours. (x + 1)/(x − 4) has no value when x = 4,
    because the denominator would be 0.
    Teacher explains: An algebraic fraction is just a fraction with letters in
    it; put in numbers to find its value: 120/40 = 3 and 120/60 = 2. For
    (x + 1)/(x − 4) try some values:
        x = 5:  (5 + 1)/(5 − 4) = 6/1 = 6
        x = 2:  (2 + 1)/(2 − 4) = 3/(−2) = −3/2
        x = 4:  (4 + 1)/(4 − 4) = 5/0   no value!
    Division by 0 is impossible: 5/0 would have to be a number that gives 5 when
    multiplied by 0, and there is none. So we write x ≠ 4 next to the fraction.
    To find forbidden values, solve denominator = 0: x − 4 = 0 gives x = 4. For
    3/(x² − 9), x² − 9 = (x + 3)(x − 3) = 0 when x = 3 or x = −3. Common
    mistake: thinking the NUMERATOR cannot be 0; (x + 1)/(x − 4) = 0 when
    x = −1, which is fine. Now you try: which values are not allowed in
    7/(2x − 6) and x/(x² − 5x)? (x ≠ 3; x ≠ 0 and x ≠ 5)

D2. Simplify 6x²y/(9xy³). Then simplify 12a³b/(8ab²).
    Answer: 6x²y/(9xy³) = 2x/(3y²); 12a³b/(8ab²) = 3a²/(2b).
    Teacher explains: Write each part as a product and cancel common factors, as
    with number fractions (12/18 = 2/3). Deal with numbers and each letter
    separately:
        6x²y       6     x²     y        2     x      1       2x
       ------  =  --- × ---- × ----  =  --- × --- × ----  =  ----
        9xy³       9     x      y³       3     1     y²       3y²
    Numbers: 6/9 = 2/3. Letter x: x²/x = x (one x cancels). Letter y:
    y/y³ = 1/y² (one y cancels, two stay below). In the same way
    12a³b/(8ab²) = (12/8)(a³/a)(b/b²) = (3/2)(a²)(1/b) = 3a²/(2b). The fraction
    is equal to the original for every allowed value: check with x = 2, y = 1:
    6 × 4 × 1/(9 × 2 × 1) = 24/18 = 4/3 and 2 × 2/(3 × 1) = 4/3. Now you try:
    15m³n²/(10mn⁴). (3m²/(2n²))

D3. Simplify (x² − 9)/(x² + 5x + 6). Ravi says (x + 3)/(x + 5) = 3/5 by
    "cancelling the x". Is he right?
    Answer: (x² − 9)/(x² + 5x + 6) = (x − 3)/(x + 2). Ravi is wrong:
    (x + 3)/(x + 5) cannot be simplified.
    Teacher explains: When there are sums, FACTORISE the top and bottom first,
    then cancel a common BRACKET:
         x² − 9              (x + 3)(x − 3)      x − 3
      -------------   =   ----------------  =  -------   (x ≠ −3, −2)
      x² + 5x + 6         (x + 2)(x + 3)       x + 2
    The bracket (x + 3) is a FACTOR of both, so it cancels. Ravi cancelled x,
    which is only a TERM (a part being added), not a factor. Test with x = 5:
    (5 + 3)/(5 + 5) = 8/10 = 4/5, not 3/5. Rule: you may cancel only what
    MULTIPLIES the whole numerator and the whole denominator. Note the
    restrictions come from the ORIGINAL denominator. Now you try:
    (x² − 4)/(x² − x − 2). ((x + 2)/(x + 1))

D4. Show that (a − b)/(b − a) = −1. Use this to simplify (6 − 2x)/(x² − 9).
    Answer: (a − b)/(b − a) = −1; (6 − 2x)/(x² − 9) = −2/(x + 3).
    Teacher explains: b − a is the negative of a − b: −(a − b) = −a + b = b − a.
    So
        (a − b)/(b − a) = (a − b)/(−(a − b)) = −1
    Check with numbers: (7 − 2)/(2 − 7) = 5/(−5) = −1. For (6 − 2x)/(x² − 9)
    factorise both: 6 − 2x = 2(3 − x) = −2(x − 3) and x² − 9 = (x + 3)(x − 3).
    Then
        −2(x − 3)/((x + 3)(x − 3)) = −2/(x + 3)
    Common mistake: cancelling (3 − x) with (x − 3) and losing the minus sign.
    Check with x = 1: (6 − 2)/(1 − 9) = 4/(−8) = −1/2 and −2/(1 + 3) = −1/2. Now
    you try: (5 − x)/(x − 5) and (x − 4)/(16 − x²). (−1 and −1/(x + 4))

D5. Work out 3/x + 5/x, (2x + 1)/5 + (x − 3)/5 and (2x + 1)/5 − (x − 3)/5.
    Answer: 8/x; (3x − 2)/5; (x + 4)/5.
    Teacher explains: With the SAME denominator, add or subtract the numerators
    and keep the denominator, just as 3/7 + 2/7 = 5/7. 3/x + 5/x = 8/x (3 lots
    of 1/x and 5 lots of 1/x make 8 lots of 1/x). For the next two, put each
    numerator in brackets first:
        (2x + 1) + (x − 3)     2x + 1 + x − 3     3x − 2
        ------------------  =  --------------  =  ------
                5                    5              5
        (2x + 1) − (x − 3)     2x + 1 − x + 3     x + 4
        ------------------  =  --------------  =  -----
                5                    5              5
    The minus sign in front of the bracket changes BOTH signs inside:
    −(x − 3) = −x + 3. The most common mistake is to write
    2x + 1 − x − 3 = x − 2. Check with x = 2: 5/5 − (−1)/5 = 6/5 and
    (2 + 4)/5 = 6/5. Now you try: (3x + 2)/4 − (x − 6)/4.
    ((2x + 8)/4 = (x + 4)/2)

D6. Work out x/3 + x/4 and (x + 1)/2 − (x − 2)/3.
    Answer: x/3 + x/4 = 7x/12; (x + 1)/2 − (x − 2)/3 = (x + 7)/6.
    Teacher explains: Different denominators: change both fractions to the LCD,
    exactly as for 1/3 + 1/4 = 4/12 + 3/12 = 7/12. LCD of 3 and 4 is 12:
        x/3 + x/4 = 4x/12 + 3x/12 = 7x/12
    For the second, LCD of 2 and 3 is 6. Multiply each WHOLE numerator:
        (x + 1)/2 − (x − 2)/3
      = 3(x + 1)/6 − 2(x − 2)/6
      = (3x + 3 − 2x + 4)/6          ((−2) × (−2) = +4)
      = (x + 7)/6
    Common mistakes: multiplying only the x (3x + 1 instead of 3x + 3), and
    forgetting that −2(x − 2) = −2x + 4. Check with x = 5: 6/2 − 3/3 = 3 − 1 = 2
    and (5 + 7)/6 = 2. Now you try: (2x − 1)/3 + (x + 2)/4. ((11x + 2)/12)

D7. Work out 2/x + 3/(2x), 1/a + 1/b and 5/x − 2/x².
    Answer: 7/(2x); (a + b)/(ab); (5x − 2)/x².
    Teacher explains: Find the LCD of algebraic denominators the same way as for
    numbers: the smallest expression that each denominator divides into.
        2/x + 3/(2x):  LCD 2x;  4/(2x) + 3/(2x) = 7/(2x)
        1/a + 1/b:     LCD ab;  b/(ab) + a/(ab) = (a + b)/(ab)
        5/x − 2/x²:    LCD x²;  5x/x² − 2/x²  = (5x − 2)/x²
    For x and x² the LCD is x² (not x³), just as the LCD of 10 and 100 is 100,
    not 1,000. Common mistake: 1/a + 1/b = 2/(a + b) or 1/(a + b). Test with
    a = 2, b = 3: 1/2 + 1/3 = 5/6, and (2 + 3)/(2 × 3) = 5/6, but
    1/(2 + 3) = 1/5. Now you try: 3/(4y) − 1/(6y) and a/b + b/a. (7/(12y) and
    (a² + b²)/(ab))

D8. Work out 1/(x + 1) + 1/(x − 1) and 3/(x + 2) − 2/(x + 3).
    Answer: 2x/((x + 1)(x − 1)) = 2x/(x² − 1); (x + 5)/((x + 2)(x + 3)).
    Teacher explains: When the denominators are different brackets with no
    common factor, the LCD is their PRODUCT, just as the LCD of 3 and 4 is 12.
    Multiply the top and bottom of each fraction by the OTHER denominator:
        1/(x + 1) + 1/(x − 1)
      = (x − 1)/((x + 1)(x − 1)) + (x + 1)/((x + 1)(x − 1))
      = (x − 1 + x + 1)/((x + 1)(x − 1)) = 2x/((x + 1)(x − 1))
        3/(x + 2) − 2/(x + 3)
      = (3(x + 3) − 2(x + 2))/((x + 2)(x + 3))
      = (3x + 9 − 2x − 4)/((x + 2)(x + 3)) = (x + 5)/((x + 2)(x + 3))
    Leave the denominator in factorised form; it shows the restrictions (x ≠ −2,
    −3) and makes simplifying easier. Check with x = 1: 3/3 − 2/4 = 1/2 and
    6/(3 × 4) = 1/2. Now you try: 2/(x − 3) + 1/(x + 2).
    ((3x + 1)/((x − 3)(x + 2)))

D9. Multiply (x² − 4)/(3x) × 6x²/(x + 2) and simplify.
    Answer: 2x(x − 2), or 2x² − 4x.
    Teacher explains: To multiply fractions, multiply tops and bottoms, but
    FACTORISE AND CANCEL FIRST so the numbers stay small:
        (x² − 4)/(3x) × 6x²/(x + 2)
      = (x + 2)(x − 2)/(3x) × 6x²/(x + 2)
      = (x − 2)/1 × 2x/1          (cancel x + 2, and 6x²/(3x) = 2x)
      = 2x(x − 2)
    Check with x = 1: (1 − 4)/3 × 6/3 = −1 × 2 = −2 and 2 × 1 × (1 − 2) = −2.
    Common mistake: cancelling x² in the first fraction with x in another term;
    only cancel factors of the WHOLE top with factors of the WHOLE bottom. Now
    you try: 5a/(a² − a) × (a − 1)/10. (1/2)

D10. Divide (x + 3)/(2x) ÷ (x² − 9)/(4x²).
     Answer: 2x/(x − 3).
     Teacher explains: Dividing by a fraction is multiplying by its reciprocal
     (keep-change-flip), as in 3/4 ÷ 1/8 = 3/4 × 8 = 6.
         (x + 3)/(2x) ÷ (x² − 9)/(4x²)
       = (x + 3)/(2x) × 4x²/(x² − 9)
       = (x + 3)/(2x) × 4x²/((x + 3)(x − 3))
       = 2x/(x − 3)                  (cancel x + 3; 4x²/(2x) = 2x)
     Flip only the SECOND fraction. Check with x = 1:
     4/2 ÷ (−8/4) = 2 ÷ (−2) = −1 and 2/(1 − 3) = −1. Now you try:
     6/(x + 1) ÷ 3/(x² − 1). (2(x − 1))

D11. Neha cycles 6 km to her aunt's house at x km/h and returns 6 km at (x + 2)
     km/h, downhill. Write her total time as a single fraction. Find the time
     when x = 4.
     Answer: Total time = 6/x + 6/(x + 2) = 12(x + 1)/(x(x + 2)) hours. When
     x = 4 it is 60/24 = 2 1/2 hours.
     Teacher explains: Time = distance ÷ speed, so the two parts take 6/x and
     6/(x + 2) hours. Add with LCD x(x + 2):
         6/x + 6/(x + 2) = (6(x + 2) + 6x)/(x(x + 2))
                         = (12x + 12)/(x(x + 2))
                         = 12(x + 1)/(x(x + 2))
     When x = 4: 12 × 5/(4 × 6) = 60/24 = 5/2 = 2 1/2 hours. Check with the
     separate parts: 6/4 + 6/6 = 1 1/2 + 1 = 2 1/2 hours. The single fraction is
     useful because now we need only one calculation for each speed. Now you
     try: a tap fills 1/x of a tank per hour and another 1/(x + 3). What
     fraction do they fill together in one hour? ((2x + 3)/(x(x + 3)))

D12. Find and correct the mistakes: (i) (x + 6)/6 = x + 1; (ii)
     1/a + 1/b = 2/(a + b); (iii) x/2 − (x − 4)/2 = −2.
     Answer: (i) (x + 6)/6 cannot be simplified to x + 1 (it equals x/6 + 1).
     (ii) 1/a + 1/b = (a + b)/(ab). (iii) x/2 − (x − 4)/2 = 4/2 = 2.
     Teacher explains: Test each claim with a number. (i) x = 6: (6 + 6)/6 = 2
     but x + 1 = 7. The 6 is not a factor of the whole numerator, so it cannot
     cancel; the correct split is (x + 6)/6 = x/6 + 6/6 = x/6 + 1. (ii) a = 1,
     b = 1: 1 + 1 = 2 but 2/(1 + 1) = 1. The correct LCD method gives
     (b + a)/(ab). (iii) The minus sign must apply to BOTH terms of x − 4:
         x/2 − (x − 4)/2 = (x − x + 4)/2 = 4/2 = 2
     The mistake gave (x − x − 4)/2 = −2. Checking with a number catches nearly
     all such errors: x = 10 gives 5 − 3 = 2. Now you try: is
     (2x + 4)/2 = x + 2? Is (x² + 4)/(x + 2) = x + 2? (yes, because
     2x + 4 = 2(x + 2); no: x = 1 gives 5/3 on the left but 3 on the right)

PART B — MATHEMATICAL ONLY
--------------------------
M1. For which values of x is each fraction NOT defined? Write "none" if every
    value is allowed.
    a) 5/(x − 4)            b) 7/x                  c) 3/(x + 2)
    d) (x + 1)/(x − 9)      e) 2/(3x − 1)           f) 3/(2x + 5)
    g) x/(x² − 9)           h) (x − 2)/(x + 7)      i) 1/(x² − 5x + 6)
    j) 4/(x² − 4x)          k) (x + 3)/(x² + 3x)    l) 9/(x² − 16)
    m) 1/(x² + 1)           n) (2x − 1)/(5 − x)     o) x/((x − 1)(x + 8))
    p) 6/(x² + 6x + 9)      q) 8/(4x² − 1)          r) (x + 5)/10
    Answers: a) x ≠ 4   b) x ≠ 0   c) x ≠ −2   d) x ≠ 9   e) x ≠ 1/3
             f) x ≠ −5/2   g) x ≠ −3, 3   h) x ≠ −7   i) x ≠ 2, 3   j) x ≠ 0, 4
             k) x ≠ 0   l) x ≠ −4, 4   m) none   n) x ≠ 5   o) x ≠ −8, 1
             p) x ≠ −3   q) x ≠ −1/2, 1/2   r) none

M2. Find the value of each fraction for the given x. Write "undefined" if the
    denominator is 0.
    a) (x + 3)/(x − 1), x = 5       b) (x + 3)/(x − 1), x = 1
    c) (x + 3)/(x − 1), x = −3      d) (x + 3)/(x − 1), x = 0
    e) 2x/(x + 4), x = 2            f) 2x/(x + 4), x = −4
    g) 2x/(x + 4), x = −2           h) (x² − 1)/x, x = 3
    i) (x² − 1)/x, x = −1           j) (x² − 1)/x, x = 0
    k) 5/(x² + 1), x = 2            l) 5/(x² + 1), x = −2
    m) (3 − x)/(x + 3), x = 3       n) (3 − x)/(x + 3), x = −1
    o) x/(x² − 4), x = 2            p) x/(x² − 4), x = 4
    q) (2x − 1)/(3x + 1), x = 1     r) (2x − 1)/(3x + 1), x = 1/2
    Answers: a) 2   b) undefined   c) 0   d) −3   e) 2/3   f) undefined   g) −2
             h) 8/3   i) 0   j) undefined   k) 1   l) 1   m) 0   n) 2
             o) undefined   p) 1/3   q) 1/4   r) 0

M3. Simplify (cancel common factors).
    a) 6x/9                b) 10a/(15b)           c) x²/x
    d) a³/a⁵               e) 6x²y/(9xy³)         f) 12a³b/(8ab²)
    g) 4m/(8m²)            h) 15p²q/(5pq)         i) 14x³/(21x)
    j) 9ab/(12a²b²)        k) 20x⁴y²/(15x²y⁵)     l) 18m²n³/(24m⁴n)
    m) 7xyz/(21xy)         n) −8a²/(12a)          o) 16x⁵/(−4x²)
    p) −25p³q/(−10pq³)     q) 36a²b²c/(48ab³c²)   r) 3x/(3x)
    s) 11y³/(22y³)         t) 45m⁵n/(27m²n²)
    Answers: a) 2x/3   b) 2a/(3b)   c) x   d) 1/a²   e) 2x/(3y²)   f) 3a²/(2b)
             g) 1/(2m)   h) 3p   i) 2x²/3   j) 3/(4ab)   k) 4x²/(3y³)
             l) 3n²/(4m²)   m) z/3   n) −2a/3   o) −4x³   p) 5p²/(2q²)
             q) 3a/(4bc)   r) 1   s) 1/2   t) 5m³/(3n)

M4. Factorise, then simplify.
    a) (3x + 6)/3              b) (5x − 10)/5
    c) (4x + 8)/(6x + 12)      d) (5x − 10)/(x − 2)
    e) (x² + 3x)/x             f) (2a + 2b)/(a + b)
    g) (6x − 9)/(4x − 6)       h) (x² − 5x)/(3x)
    i) (7m + 14)/(m + 2)       j) (3a − 12)/(a² − 4a)
    k) (ab + ac)/(ab)          l) (10x + 15)/(20x + 30)
    m) (x² + x)/(x² − x)       n) (4y − 8)/(8y)
    o) (p² + 3p)/(p² + 2p)     p) 2x/(2x + 6)
    q) (9x² − 3x)/(3x)         r) (12a − 18)/(8a − 12)
    s) (x + 4)/(3x + 12)       t) (6m² + 9m)/(4m + 6)
    Answers: a) x + 2   b) x − 2   c) 2/3   d) 5   e) x + 3   f) 2   g) 3/2
             h) (x − 5)/3   i) 7   j) 3/a   k) (b + c)/b   l) 1/2
             m) (x + 1)/(x − 1)   n) (y − 2)/(2y)   o) (p + 3)/(p + 2)
             p) x/(x + 3)   q) 3x − 1   r) 3/2   s) 1/3   t) 3m/2

M5. Factorise, then simplify. Two of them cannot be simplified: say which.
    a) (x² − 9)/(x² + 5x + 6)         b) (x² − 4)/(x² − x − 2)
    c) (x² − 1)/(x + 1)               d) (x² + 5x + 6)/(x + 3)
    e) (x − 3)/(x² − 9)               f) (x² − 16)/(x² − 8x + 16)
    g) (a − b)/(b − a)                h) (5 − x)/(x − 5)
    i) (6 − 2x)/(x² − 9)              j) (x − 4)/(16 − x²)
    k) (x² + 7x + 12)/(x² + 5x + 4)   l) (x² − x − 6)/(x² − 4)
    m) (2x² + x − 3)/(x² − 1)         n) (x² + 2x)/(x² − 4)
    o) (3x² − 12)/(6x + 12)           p) (x² − 10x + 25)/(x² − 25)
    q) (x + 3)/(x + 5)                r) (x² + 4)/(x + 2)
    s) (2x² − 8)/(x² + 4x + 4)        t) (9 − x²)/(x² − x − 6)
    Answers: a) (x − 3)/(x + 2)   b) (x + 2)/(x + 1)   c) x − 1   d) x + 2
             e) 1/(x + 3)   f) (x + 4)/(x − 4)   g) −1   h) −1   i) −2/(x + 3)
             j) −1/(x + 4)   k) (x + 3)/(x + 1)   l) (x − 3)/(x − 2)
             m) (2x + 3)/(x + 1)   n) x/(x − 2)   o) (x − 2)/2
             p) (x − 5)/(x + 5)   q) cannot be simplified
             r) cannot be simplified   s) 2(x − 2)/(x + 2)   t) −(x + 3)/(x + 2)

M6. Add or subtract (same denominators). Simplify where you can.
    a) 3/x + 5/x
    b) 7/a − 2/a
    c) x/5 + 2x/5
    d) 3y/8 − y/8
    e) (x + 1)/3 + x/3
    f) (2x + 1)/5 + (x − 3)/5
    g) (2x + 1)/5 − (x − 3)/5
    h) (3x + 2)/4 − (x − 6)/4
    i) 5/(x + 1) + 3/(x + 1)
    j) (x + 4)/(x − 2) − (x − 1)/(x − 2)
    k) (4a − 3)/7 − (2a − 5)/7
    l) x/(x + 3) + 3/(x + 3)
    m) (x² + 1)/x − 1/x
    n) (5m + 2)/6 − (m + 8)/6
    o) 2x/(x − 1) − 2/(x − 1)
    p) (7p − 1)/(2p) + (p + 1)/(2p)
    q) (x + 5)/9 − (x − 4)/9
    r) (3 − x)/(x + 2) + (2x − 1)/(x + 2)
    s) x²/(x − 4) − 16/(x − 4)
    t) (a + b)/c − (a − b)/c
    Answers: a) 8/x   b) 5/a   c) 3x/5   d) y/4   e) (2x + 1)/3   f) (3x − 2)/5
             g) (x + 4)/5   h) (x + 4)/2   i) 8/(x + 1)   j) 5/(x − 2)
             k) 2(a + 1)/7   l) 1   m) x   n) (2m − 3)/3   o) 2   p) 4   q) 1
             r) 1   s) x + 4   t) 2b/c

M7. Add or subtract (use the LCD of the numbers).
    a) x/3 + x/4                  b) x/2 − x/5
    c) 2x/3 + x/6                 d) 3a/4 − a/8
    e) x/2 + x/3 + x/6            f) (x + 1)/2 + (x + 3)/4
    g) (x + 1)/2 − (x − 2)/3      h) (2x − 1)/3 + (x + 2)/4
    i) (3x + 1)/5 − (x − 2)/10    j) x + x/3
    k) (y − 1)/4 − (y + 1)/6      l) (5x − 2)/6 + (x + 4)/9
    m) 2 − (x − 1)/3              n) (a + 2)/3 − (a − 1)/2
    o) (4m − 3)/8 + (m + 1)/12    p) (x − 3)/4 − (x − 7)/4
    q) x/7 + (x − 7)/14           r) (2p + 5)/10 − (p − 5)/15
    s) 3x/4 − (x + 2)/3 + 1/6     t) (x + 1)/2 + (x + 1)/3
